python3 - <<'E'
import re,html
h=open('tcs.html').read()
for sec in ['quarters','profit-loss','balance-sheet','cash-flow','ratios']:
    i=h.find(f'id="{sec}"'); j=h.find('</section>',i); s=h[i:j]
    rows=re.findall(r'<tr[^>]*>(.*?)</tr>',s,re.S)
    print('==',sec,len(rows))
    for r in rows[:30]:
        cells=[re.sub(r'\s+',' ',html.unescape(re.sub(r'<[^>]+>','',c))).strip() for c in re.findall(r'<t[hd][^>]*>(.*?)</t[hd]>',r,re.S)]
        print(cells[:1]+cells[-4:])
E

Write — /scratch/ee/btech/ee3230385/finn/netlify/lib/fundamentals.js
/scratch/ee/btech/ee3230385/finn/netlify/lib/fundamentals.js
// Deterministic analysis of the standardised statements. Every flag carries the exact numbers it was
// computed from ("evidence") and the rule that fired ("rule"), so the investor can audit the judgement.
// Banks/NBFCs have a different statement shape; we detect it and say so rather than forcing EBITDA.

const pick = (t, names) => {
  if (!t) return null;
  for (const n of names) if (t.rows[n]) return t.rows[n];
  return null;
};
const last = (a, k = 1) => (a ? a[a.length - k] : null);

// Two-level cache: in-memory (per warm function instance) + Netlify Blobs (shared, persistent).
// Every LLM output is keyed by the source document URL, so a filing/transcript is paid for once, ever.
import { getStore } from "@netlify/blobs";

const mem = new Map();
let store = null;
function blobs() {
  if (store !== null) return store;
  try { store = getStore({ name: "finn-cache", consistency: "eventual" }); } catch { store = false; }
  return store;
}

    if (!s) return null;

export const json = (data, status = 200, maxAge = 0) =>
  new Response(JSON.stringify(data), { status, headers: { "content-type": "application/json", "cache-control": maxAge ? `public, max-age=${maxAge}` : "no-store" } });
export const fail = (e) => json({ error: e.message || String(e), suggestions: e.suggestions || undefined }, e.status || 500);
export const sym = (s) => (s || "").toUpperCase().replace(/[^A-Z0-9&_-]/g, "").slice(0, 20);
E

function reactionDay(iso) {
  const ist = new Date(new Date(iso).getTime() + 5.5 * 3600e3);

  if (afterClose) ist.setUTCDate(ist.getUTCDate() + 1);

}''')

Bash — Warm caches for demo research companies
Warm caches for demo research companies
$ P=/tmp/claude-110131/-home-ee-btech-ee3230385/077de908-ddfb-4ce2-aed9-7afd9f94acb7/scratchpad; cat > $P/warm.sh <<'E'
#!/bin/bash
S=https://finn-investor.netlify.app
for s in "$@"; do
  a=$(curl -s -m 120 -o /dev/null -w "%{http_code}/%{time_total}" "$S/api/company?symbol=$s")
  b=$(curl -s -m 120 -o /dev/null -w "%{http_code}/%{time_total}" "$S/api/brief?symbol=$s&part=business")
  c=$(curl -s -m 150 -o /dev/null -w "%{http_code}/%{time_total}" "$S/api/ledger?symbol=$s&judge=0")
  d=$(curl -s -m 150 -o /dev/null -w "%{http_code}/%{time_total}" "$S/api/ledger?symbol=$s&judge=1")
  e=$(curl -s -m 150 -o /dev/null -w "%{http_code}/%{time_total}" "$S/api/brief?symbol=$s&part=bullbear")
  echo "$s company=$a biz=$b l0=$c l1=$d bb=$e"
done
E
chmod +x $P/warm.sh; nohup nice $P/warm.sh POLYCAB KAYNES ASIANPAINT HAL JUBLFOOD > $P/warm.log 2>&1 &
echo started

// Does the triage score work? Score every filing of the last N days for the portfolio WITHOUT the
// market-reaction term (that would be circular), then look at the stock's next-session move in units
// of its own volatility. If the ranking carries information, "Needs you" filings should be followed
// by bigger moves than "Filed away" ones.
import { nseAnnouncements, dedupe } from "./exchanges.js";
import { classify, score } from "./classify.js";
import { companyContext } from "./digest.js";
import { memo } from "./cache.js";

const reactionDay = (iso) => {
  const ist = new Date(new Date(iso).getTime() + 5.5 * 3600e3);
  if (ist.getUTCHours() * 60 + ist.getUTCMinutes() > 15 * 60 + 30) ist.setUTCDate(ist.getUTCDate() + 1);
  return ist.toISOString().slice(0, 10);
};

  if (!dir) return null;

}
'''

Bash — Dump a transcript to study its structure
Dump a transcript to study its structure
$ cd /scratch/ee/btech/ee3230385/finn && export PATH=/scratch/ee/btech/ee3230385/envs/node/bin:$PATH && cat > scripts/dump_transcript.mjs <<'E'
import { fetchCompany } from "../netlify/lib/screener.js";
import { fetchPdfPages } from "../netlify/lib/pdf.js";
import { writeFile } from "node:fs/promises";
const c = await fetchCompany(process.argv[2]);

await writeFile(process.argv[3], JSON.stringify(d));
console.log(call.period, d.nPages, d.pages.slice(0, 3).map((p) => p.slice(0, 700)).join("\n-----\n"));
E

Bash — Candour endpoint; feed into bull/bear evidence
Candour endpoint; feed into bull/bear evidence
$ cd /scratch/ee/btech/ee3230385/finn && cat > netlify/functions/candour.mjs <<'E'
import { companyCore } from "../lib/research.js";
import { candour } from "../lib/discourse.js";
import { pickCalls } from "../lib/ledger.js";
import { memo } from "../lib/cache.js";
import { json, fail, sym } from "../lib/http.js";
// No model involved: lexicon + structure over the last four transcripts. Cached until a new call is filed.
export default async (req) => {
  const s = sym(new URL(req.url).searchParams.get("symbol"));
  if (!s) return json({ error: "symbol required" }, 400);
  try {
    const { c } = await companyCore(s);
    const calls = pickCalls(c, 4);
    return json(await memo(`candour:v1:${c.symbol}:${calls[0]?.transcript || "none"}`, 7 * 864e5, () => candour(c, calls)));
  } catch (e) { return fail(e); }
};
export const config = { path: "/api/candour" };
E
cat > api/candour.js <<'E'

import json
d=json.load(open('eval/nonanswers_raw.json'))
false={0:"caveat inside a substantive explanation",6:"partial answer: gave the 2-month figure",12:"answered (explained why not a run-rate)"}
labels=[{"i":i,"company":x["s"],"call":x["period"],"kind":x["kind"],"phrase":x["phrase"],"answer":x["answer"],"label":"false_positive" if i in false else "non_answer","note":false.get(i,"")} for i,x in enumerate(d["detections"])]
json.dump(labels,open('eval/nonanswers_labelled.json','w'),indent=1)
tp=sum(l["label"]=="non_answer" for l in labels); print("precision", tp, "/", len(labels), "=", round(tp/len(labels)*100,1))
from collections import Counter
print(Counter((l["kind"],l["label"]) for l in labels))

# Evaluations and ablations

Every number FINN shows is either computed in code or checked against its source. This folder records how those checks were evaluated, including what didn't work.

## 1. Non-answer detector (Candour tab)

**Method.** The detector pairs each analyst question with the management turn that follows. It flags a pair when the answer matches a phrase lexicon in three categories:

// Management credibility as a probability, not an adjective.
// Hutton & Stocken (2009): investors weight a manager's forecast by that manager's past accuracy.
// We make the weight explicit: each checkable promise is a Bernoulli trial (kept / not kept); with a
// uniform Beta(1,1) prior the posterior after k of n is Beta(1+k, 1+n−k). Its mean is the chance the
// *next* promise is kept, and its 80% interval says how much the record can be trusted (few promises
// → wide interval). Partial outcomes count half. Style labels come from the goalpost history
// (Richardson, Teoh & Wysocki 2004: guidance is walked down to beatable levels).

const KEPT = { delivered: 1, beat: 1, "on track": 1, partly: 0.5, behind: 0, missed: 0 };

// regularized incomplete beta I_x(a,b) via continued fraction (Numerical Recipes 6.4) … [5174 more chars]

  method: 'Beta(1,1) prior updated by kept/not-kept promises (partly = ½); pNext = posterior mean, interval = 10th–90th percentile'

Bash — Make wide tables horizontally scrollable
Make wide tables horizontally scrollable

import re
p='src/index.css'; s=open(p).read()
s+="\n.xscroll { overflow-x: auto; -webkit-overflow-scrolling: touch; margin: 0 -2px; padding: 0 2px; }\n.xscroll table.fig td:first-child, .xscroll table.fig th:first-child { position: sticky; left: 0; background: var(--surface); min-width: 120px; }\n"

p='src/Research.jsx'; s=open(p).read()
n=0
def wrap(m):
    global n; n+=1
    return '<div className="xscroll">'+m.group(0)+'</div>'
s=re.sub(r'<table className="fig( mt\d+)?">.*?</table>', wrap, s, flags=re.S)
open(p,'w').write(s); print("wrapped", n)
E

Claude · 15 Nov 04:56
Progress report for when you're back; work continues after this. Everything is committed locally (25 commits):

**New tonight, all built and tested locally with no AI cost:**
- **Candour:** detects when management dodges analyst questions, applying a published method (Gow et al. 2021). Every dodge is shown verbatim with its page.
  - Also measures tone and uncertainty, flags *narrative vs numbers* divergence, and shows analyst praise, a dodge rate on hard vs routine questions, and "what the street kept asking about".
- **Portfolio candour league:** all holdings ranked by how often management dodged on the latest call. SUNPHARMA 19%, POLYCAB 18%; L&T and Reliance about 0%.
- **Chance the next promise is kept:** turns the Promise Ledger into a probability with an uncertainty range, plus style labels like "walks down, then beats".
- **Promises × Candour:** each promise shows whether analysts asked about it and got dodged.
- **Push additions:**
  - a new transcript filing instantly shows "dodged 5 of 28, up from the last call";
  - duplicate filings from one event merge into one card;
  - a check against SEBI's 5-day transcript rule.
- **Bugs fixed:**
  - a page crash when opening the Candour tab directly;
  - Maruti's parent-company results being shown as its own;
  - a transcript parser that handled only some layouts (now 9 transcripts across 5 layouts, with a regression set).

**Measured honestly (`eval/README.md`):**
- **Dodge detector:** 86–92% precision, ~55–60% recall.
- **Script-echo idea:** tested and dropped.
- **Event study (n=40):** inconclusive, not claimed.

**Testing:** 16 unit tests, a parser regression set, and a UI smoke test across all screens in light and dark mode. All pass.

**Two things only you can do (2 minutes each):**
1. **Vercel token:** create one at https://vercel.com/account/settings/tokens, then deploy and pre-warm with one command (`scripts/warm.mjs`).
2. **GitHub classic token:** https://github.com/settings/tokens/new, tick `repo`, and paste the `ghp_…` token.

I'm continuing to improve things locally.

Bash — Auditor endpoint, UI card, evidence feed; test
Auditor endpoint, UI card, evidence feed; test
$ cd /scratch/ee/btech/ee3230385/finn && cat > netlify/functions/auditor.mjs <<'E'
import { companyCore } from "../lib/research.js";
import { auditorFindings } from "../lib/auditor.js";
import { memo } from "../lib/cache.js";
import { json, fail, sym } from "../lib/http.js";
// What the auditor said in the latest annual report (no model; cached per report).
export default async (req) => {
  const s = sym(new URL(req.url).searchParams.get("symbol"));
  if (!s) return json({ error: "symbol required" }, 400);
  try {
    const { c } = await companyCore(s);
    const ar = c.docs.annualReports[0];
    return json(await memo(`auditor:v1:${ar?.url || s}`, Infinity, () => auditorFindings(ar)));
  } catch (e) { return fail(e); }
};
export const config = { path: "/api/auditor" };
E

Claude · 15 Nov 05:03
Your usage limit was reached, so I'm stopping here. Everything is committed locally (33 commits in `~/ENAM/finn`, which points to `/scratch/ee/btech/ee3230385/finn`). The live site is down (Netlify paused it when the free credits ran out), and the GitHub repo is still empty because no push has succeeded.

**What's working, tested locally (18/18 unit tests pass; the screen-by-screen phone-browser check has no errors):**
- **Push:**
  - ranked filing digest with the reasons behind each score, deduplicated so one event shows as one card;
  - price/volume alerts;
  - a "Send to WhatsApp" brief and 60-second Listen mode;
  - when a company files a call transcript, an instant read of how many analyst questions were dodged, plus a check against SEBI's 5-working-day transcript deadline.
- **Research:**
  - **Promise Ledger:** each promise checked against reported numbers, and a "chance the next promise is kept" probability.
  - **Candour:** questions management dodged, tone, analyst praise, topics analysts kept asking about, and a ranking of all holdings by dodging.
  - **What the auditor said** (new, built at the end): reads the latest annual report and found the auditor's reports in 20 of 21. It flags real "emphasis of matter" notes, e.g. Suzlon's restated prior-year figures and notes at Maruti and Dixon.
  - Revenue mix, cash quality, balance sheet, and a bull/bear case that may only cite verified facts.
- **Measured honestly** (write-up in `eval/README.md`):
  - The dodge detector is 86–92% precise and finds about 55–60% of dodges.
  - The triage score, checked on 6 months it wasn't tuned on: "Needs you" filings were followed by a big price move 27% of the time vs 11% for routine ones.
  - Whether dodging predicts stock returns is inconclusive on 40 calls, and I don't claim it does.
- **Docs:** README, build log, a draft covering email (`SUBMISSION_EMAIL.md`), and an in-app "How FINN works" sheet.

**What's left:**
1. **Your Vercel token**, then I deploy and pre-load the demo pages (`node scripts/warm.mjs <url>`). The parts that use the AI model (ledger judging, filing summaries, bull/bear) haven't been tested on Vercel yet.
2. **A GitHub classic token** with `repo` scope (from https://github.com/settings/tokens/new), then I push.
3. **Not yet checked by hand:** the Maruti and Dixon auditor flags, and a "going-concern" flag on one company the scan only listed by filename. These should be checked before the submission mentions them. Also one annual report (Bajaj Finance) where the auditor's report still isn't found.
4. **Before you send:** export this session for the AI build history, put the live URL into `SUBMISSION_EMAIL.md`, and revoke both GitHub tokens, which appear in this transcript.

Claude · 15 Nov 15:59
I've moved FINN's reasoning steps to **Claude Opus 5.5**, the model I run on, and kept Haiku 5.5 for reading filings and transcripts. Opus now judges promises across calls and writes the bull and bear cases. The code, the app's "How FINN works" and Rules screens, the README, the build log and the draft email all say so. Neither Opus call has actually run yet: the live site is still paused and there's no AI access locally. A new company now costs about ₹9 of AI the first time instead of about ₹5, then nothing, because results are cached; that figure is an estimate from list prices, not measured.

I also hand-checked all 9 flags from the auditor's-report scan, which read the auditor's section of each latest annual report:
- **8 are genuine "emphasis of matter" notes:** Suzlon's restated prior-year figures, Maruti's obligation for scrapping end-of-life vehicles that it can't estimate, Eternal's GST orders on delivery charges, Dixon's ₹1,110 cr of PLI incentive income booked at a subsidiary, and two each for Maruti and HAL.
- **1 was misleading:** a "going concern" doubt pinned on HAL actually belonged to a small group company (a ₹12.8 lakh shortfall). FINN now labels these as group-company items, and a test covers it.

All 19 unit tests pass, and everything is committed locally.

**Still needed from you to submit:**
1. **A Vercel token** (https://vercel.com/account/settings/tokens). Then I deploy, pre-load the demo pages, and test the Opus calls end to end for the first time.
2. **A GitHub classic token** with `repo` ticked (https://github.com/settings/tokens/new). Then I push the code.
